| 2026-11-19 20:30 UTC | 2026-11-19 22:30 UTC | Basketball - Euroleague: Milan - Fenerbahce | The Euroleague is a competition that began its journey into the world of European basketball under the name European Champions Cup way back in 1958, and the first trophy was won by the ASK Riga team from the then USSR, i.e. today's Latvia. FIBA had the main say in this competition until 2000 when the clubs took control of the competition through their association Eurolegaue Basketball. The format of the competition has changed throughout history and included a group stage, followed by eliminations in the playoffs. The winner of this competition is decided at the final tournament called the Final Four. The most successful clubs in this competition are Real Madrid, CSKA Moscow, Maccabi Tel-Aviv, Panathinaikos, Varese, Olympiakos. |

In addition to the top teams, the Spanish league is also characterized by the extremely technical mentality of the game of most clubs with the aim of outplaying the opponent. |
| 2026-11-22 08:00 UTC | 2026-11-22 10:00 UTC | Fudbal - Španska liga: Sevilla - Betis 21.11. | Primera or popularly La Liga is the first Spanish football league. With as many as 22 European Championship titles won, the Spanish Premier League has been considered the best European football league for years. Founded in 1929, Primera has 20 teams that try to collect the most points in the 38 rounds of the season. Real Madrid managed to do that the most times, while the second most successful team is Barcelona. In addition to the top teams, the Spanish league is also characterized by the extremely technical mentality of the game of most clubs with the aim of outplaying the opponent. |
